⏺ User approved Claude's plan
  ⎿  Plan saved to: ~/.claude/plans/rippling-churning-reddy.md · /plan to edit
     Calculate tab — Plan

     Context

     Add a new Calculate tab that lets a visitor type any human PDB ID and get
     back the same panel the auto "example of the week" produces — KPIs, stats,
     key facts, the Cα-deviation ribbon SVG, the interactive 3D viewer, and the
     structure / why-wrong write-up — computed on demand, with no Claude/LLM 
     usage (the panel prose is the deterministic template in examples_auto.py).

     The entry must satisfy three criteria: (1) exists in the PDB, (2) is human,
     (3) has an AlphaFold model covering the entity's sequence. The input
     tab-autocompletes only to qualifying entries; if a submitted entry fails,
     the user is told the specific reason.

     Decisions confirmed with the user:
     - Strict precomputed index for autocomplete — approved conditional on it 
     auto-rebuilding weekly. This is feasible (see below): 82,796 entities /
     6,564 UniProts already in entities seed it for free; the weekly run()
     already resolves each week's new human entities, so the weekly refresh is
     nearly free; only a one-off pre-cutoff sweep is new work.
     - Scope: any human PDB with an AF model (not just post-cutoff). Pre-cutoff
     entries compute normally but the panel shows a note that AlphaFold may have
     trained on the structure (so it is not a blind test).

     Nearly everything is wiring existing pieces: pipeline.process_entity()
     already does the full on-demand core (validate single-UniProt → resolve AF
     fragment → download → compare.compare → ribbon.render_and_store_all →
     store), and examples_auto.build() turns the stored row into the panel.

     Components

     1. Strict autocomplete index (calculate_index) + weekly auto-rebuild

     - New table calculate_index(entity_id PK, entry_id, uniprot, gene, title, 
     deposit_date, post_cutoff INT, indexed_at) and a small
     af_uniprot_cache(uniprot PK, has_model INT, checked_at) so
     AlphaFold-availability is queried once per UniProt, not per entity.
     - New module alphafraud/calc_index.py:
       - seed_from_entities() — one SELECT of qualifying rows already in
     entities (uniprot IS NOT NULL AND af_entry_id IS NOT NULL) → upsert into
     the index (post_cutoff=1). Instant; covers 82,796 entities.
       - sweep_pre_cutoff() — RCSB search for human protein entities deposited
     before the cutoff, paginated; pdb.fetch_entity_metadata() in batches of 100
     → UniProt per entity; dedup UniProt → afdb.fetch_predictions(uniprot)
     (cached in af_uniprot_cache, empty list ⇒ no model) → upsert qualifiers
     (post_cutoff=0). One-off, ~15–20 min.
       - refresh_weekly(new_ids, metas) — upsert the week's qualifying human
     entities the run already resolved.
     - Reuse/extend pdb.py: the search plumbing (post_json, pagination in
     search_new_human_entities) is reused; add a variant that does not floor the
     date range at the cutoff (_search_query currently does max(since, 
     AF_TRAINING_CUTOFF)), e.g. search_human_entities(since, until, 
     enforce_cutoff=False).
     - AlphaFraud.py subcommands: calc-index --seed and calc-index --rebuild
     (seed + pre-cutoff sweep).
     - Hook calc_index.refresh_weekly(...) into pipeline.run() right beside the
     existing db.rebuild_weekly_examples() call, so the index stays current with
     zero extra fetches. → answers the user's feasibility condition: yes, it 
     auto-rebuilds each week.

     Strictness note: the index guarantees exists + human +
     single-UniProt-with-an-AF-model. The exact "fragment covers the resolved
     residues" is confirmed at submit by pipeline._resolve_fragment (the rare
     mismatch surfaces as a reason), because per-entity coverage confirmation
     would require downloading every AF model at index time.

     2. Autocomplete endpoint + UI

     - webapp.py: GET /api/calculate/suggest?q=<prefix> → db.index_suggest(q, 
     limit=8) → JSON [{entity_id, entry_id, label: "GENE — title", 
     post_cutoff}]. Prefix-match on entry_id, plus gene/title contains.
     - templates/calculate.html: the tab page — search input, a results/error
     container, and the panel mount point.
     - static/app.js: debounced fetch, keyboard up/down, Tab-to-complete to the
     top suggestion, Enter/click to submit.
     - base.html: add the Calculate nav link.

     3. Validation + on-demand compute (no LLM)

     - webapp.py: POST /calculate/run {pdb_id|entity_id}:
       a. Normalise to an entity_id — if a 4-char PDB id, pick its primary
     human-UniProt entity from the index; if several, return a small chooser
     list.
       b. If already compared/screened in the corpus → {status: "ready", 
     entity_id} (instant, reuse).
       c. Else validate live via pdb.fetch_entity_metadata([id]) and map
     failures to reasons: not found → "No such PDB entry"; not human → "not a
     human structure"; not meta.has_single_uniprot → "no single UniProt mapping
     (antibody / fusion / chimera)"; afdb.fetch_predictions empty → "no
     AlphaFold model for UniProt X"; fragment doesn't cover span → "AlphaFold
     model doesn't cover the resolved region"; too large → the existing
     _too_large_reason.
       d. If valid → write a status='computing' placeholder, spawn a subprocess
     AlphaFraud.py calculate <entity_id> (subprocess, not a thread — avoids the
     known biotite/tmtools C-extension thread-safety issues), return {status: 
     "computing", entity_id}.
     - GET /api/calculate/status/<entity_id> → polls the row: computing | ready 
     | error(reason).
     - GET /calculate/panel/<entity_id> → renders the panel HTML fragment
     (below) for the frontend to inject.
     - New alphafraud/calculate.py (or a pipeline function) compute(entity_id):
     meta = pdb.fetch_entity_metadata([entity_id]); run_id = a persistent
     kind="calculate" run (db.start_run); pipeline.process_entity(entity_id, 
     meta, run_id); on success annotate.fetch([entity]) so the panel has
     CATH/citation fields. Store with status='calculated' (see isolation).
     - AlphaFraud.py calculate <entity_id> subcommand — the subprocess entry
     point (also usable from the CLI).

     4. Panel rendering — identical to the auto example, no Claude

     - Extract the joined-row helper already embedded in
     db.rebuild_weekly_examples (the _AUTO_SRC_COLS entity+annotation+metrics
     join) into db.auto_source_row(entity_id).
     - /calculate/panel/<id>: row = db.auto_source_row(id) → data = 
     examples_auto.build(row) → reuse the webapp's _enrich_example(data) →
     render _expanel.html (the existing partial). Same KPIs / key facts / ribbon
     / 3D viewer / structure + why-wrong sections, no biology. If
     post_cutoff=0, pass a flag so the template shows the "AlphaFold may have
     seen this in training" note.

     5. Storage isolation

     - Calculated entities live in entities under the kind="calculate" run but
     with status='calculated' — outside ANALYSED = status IN 
     ('screened','compared'), so they are automatically excluded from every
     aggregate (home stats, leaderboard, archive, analysis, weekly-examples)
     with no query changes. get_entity, the /ribbon /coords /ghost routes,
     family_summary, and examples_auto.build all key off entity_id and still
     work. process_entity currently hard-codes status:"compared" in its upsert —
     add a status param (default "compared") so the calculate path sets
     "calculated".
     - Reuse: if the corpus already has the entity (compared/screened), render
     it directly — never recompute.

     6. Guards (small droplet, 3.8 GB RAM)

     - Persisted results ⇒ repeat requests are instant.
     - Global concurrency cap (e.g. ≤ 2 status='computing' at once) → otherwise
     return "busy, try again shortly".
     - Light per-IP throttle via the existing hashed-IP visits table.
     - RAM is already protected by _too_large inside process_entity.

     Files

     - New: alphafraud/calc_index.py, alphafraud/calculate.py,
     alphafraud/templates/calculate.html.
     - Modify: alphafraud/db.py (index + af-cache schema/queries,
     auto_source_row, status isolation), alphafraud/pdb.py
     (search_human_entities no-cutoff variant), alphafraud/pipeline.py
     (process_entity status param; refresh_weekly hook in run()),
     alphafraud/webapp.py (5 routes + reuse _enrich_example),
     alphafraud/static/app.js (autocomplete + submit/poll/inject),
     alphafraud/templates/base.html (nav link), AlphaFraud.py (calc-index,
     calculate subcommands).

     Verification

     1. AlphaFraud.py calc-index --seed then --rebuild; confirm calculate_index
     row count (~tens of thousands) and that a known pre-cutoff human PDB (e.g.
     1CRN… pick a real human one) and a post-cutoff one (6TT5) both appear.
     2. AlphaFraud.py calculate 6TT5_1 locally → entity stored
     status='calculated', ribbon assets rendered, examples_auto.build yields a
     full panel.
     3. serve → /calculate: typing prefixes autocompletes to qualifying entries;
     Tab completes; submitting 6TT5 shows the Artemis-style panel; submitting
     an antibody/non-human/no-AF PDB shows the correct reason.
     4. Confirm isolation: /, /leaderboard, /analysis, /archive KPIs/counts are
     unchanged after calculating several entries (calculated rows excluded).
     5. On the droplet after deploy: trigger a weekly run (or calc-index
     refresh) and confirm new week's entities enter the index automatically.

     Open notes

     - Autocomplete is "strict" to exists + human + UniProt-with-AF-model; exact
     per-residue AF coverage is a submit-time confirmation (rare failure, with
     a reason). Flagged so the user can decide if that's strict enough.
     - Multi-entity PDB IDs default to the primary human-UniProt entity, with a
     chooser when several qualify.

      547 +def auto_source_row(entity_id: str):

      557 +def index_upsert(rows: list[dict]) -> int:                    
      558 +    """Upsert qualifying entities into the autocomplete index.
          + Each row: entity_id, entry_id,                               
      559 +    uniprot, gene, title, deposit_date, post_cutoff."""       
      560 +    if not rows:

      564 +        _ensure_calc(conn)                                    
      565 +        conn.executemany(                                     
      566 +            "INSERT INTO calculate_index (entity_id, entry_id,
          + uniprot, gene, title, deposit_date, "                        
      567 +            "post_cutoff, indexed_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
          + "

          + r.get("gene"), r.get("title"),                               
      572 +              r.get("deposit_date"), r.get("post_cutoff"), now
          +) for r in rows])                                             
      573 +    return len(rows)                                          
      574 +

      576 +def seed_index_from_entities() -> int:                        
      577 +    """Seed the index from corpus entities that already have a
          + UniProt + AlphaFold model (proven                            
      578 +    qualifying by the pipeline). post_cutoff is derived from t
          +he deposit date."""                                           
      579 +    cutoff = config.AF_TRAINING_CUTOFF.isoformat()            
      580 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      581 +        _ensure_calc(conn)                                    
      582 +        rows = conn.execute(                                  
      583 +            "SELECT entity_id, entry_id, uniprot, uniprot_name
          + gene, description title, deposit_date, "                     
      584 +            "CASE WHEN substr(deposit_date,1,10) >= ? THEN 1 E
          +LSE 0 END post_cutoff "                                       
      585 +            "FROM entities WHERE uniprot IS NOT NULL AND af_en
          +try_id IS NOT NULL "                                          
      586 +            "AND status IN ('screened','compared')", (cutoff,)
          +).fetchall()                                                  
      587 +    return index_upsert([dict(r) for r in rows])              
      588 +                                                              
      589 +                                                              
      590 +def index_count() -> int:                                     
      591 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      592 +        _ensure_calc(conn)                                    
      593 +        return conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM calculate_in
          +dex").fetchone()[0]                                           
      594 +                                                              
      595 +                                                              
      596 +def index_suggest(prefix: str, limit: int = 8) -> list[dict]: 
      597 +    """Autocomplete: qualifying entries whose PDB id starts wi
          +th `prefix` (preferred) or whose                              
      598 +    gene/title contains it. Deduplicated to one row per PDB en
          +try (the lowest entity number)."""                            
      599 +    p = (prefix or "").strip().lower()                        
      600 +    if not p:                                                 
      601 +        return []                                             
      602 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      603 +        _ensure_calc(conn)                                    
      604 +        rows = conn.execute(                                  
      605 +            "SELECT entity_id, entry_id, uniprot, gene, title,
          + post_cutoff, "                                               
      606 +            "       (lower(entry_id) LIKE ?) AS id_hit "      
      607 +            "FROM calculate_index "                           
      608 +            "WHERE lower(entry_id) LIKE ? OR lower(gene) LIKE 
          +? OR lower(title) LIKE ? "                                    
      609 +            "ORDER BY id_hit DESC, entry_id, entity_id LIMIT 2
          +00",                                                          
      610 +            (p + "%", p + "%", "%" + p + "%", "%" + p + "%")).
          +fetchall()                                                    
      611 +    seen, out = set(), []                                     
      612 +    for r in rows:                                   # one sug
          +gestion per PDB entry                                         
      613 +        if r["entry_id"] in seen:                             
      614 +            continue                                          
      615 +        seen.add(r["entry_id"])                               
      616 +        out.append(dict(r))                                   
      617 +        if len(out) >= limit:                                 
      618 +            break                                             
      619 +    return out                                                
      620 +                                                              
      621 +                                                              
      622 +def index_entities_for_entry(entry_id: str) -> list[dict]:    
      623 +    """All qualifying entities of a PDB entry (to resolve a ba
          +re 4-char id / offer a chooser)."""                           
      624 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      625 +        _ensure_calc(conn)                                    
      626 +        rows = conn.execute(                                  
      627 +            "SELECT * FROM calculate_index WHERE lower(entry_i
          +d)=? ORDER BY entity_id",                                     
      628 +            (entry_id.lower(),)).fetchall()                   
      629 +        return [dict(r) for r in rows]                        
      630 +                                                              
      631 +                                                              
      632 +def af_cache_get(uniprot: str) -> Optional[bool]:             
      633 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      634 +        _ensure_calc(conn)                                    
      635 +        r = conn.execute("SELECT has_model FROM af_uniprot_cac
          +he WHERE uniprot=?", (uniprot,)).fetchone()                   
      636 +        return None if r is None else bool(r["has_model"])    
      637 +                                                              
      638 +                                                              
      639 +def af_cache_set(uniprot: str, has_model: bool) -> None:      
      640 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      641 +        _ensure_calc(conn)                                    
      642 +        conn.execute(                                         
      643 +            "INSERT INTO af_uniprot_cache (uniprot, has_model,
          + checked_at) VALUES (?,?,?) "                                 
      644 +            "ON CONFLICT(uniprot) DO UPDATE SET has_model=excl
          +uded.has_model, checked_at=excluded.checked_at",              
      645 +            (uniprot, 1 if has_model else 0, _now()))         
      646 +                                                              
      647 +                                                              
      648 +def calculate_run_id() -> int:                                
      649 +    """The singleton run that owns on-demand 'Calculate' entit
          +ies (kind='calculate', so they are                            
      650 +    excluded from every 'weekly'/'backfill' aggregate)."""    
      651 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      652 +        r = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM runs WHERE kind='calc
          +ulate' ORDER BY id LIMIT 1").fetchone()                       
      653 +        if r:                                                 
      654 +            return r["id"]                                    
      655 +    return start_run("calculate", "", "", kind="calculate")   
      656 +                                                              
      657 +                                                              
      658 +def calc_status(entity_id: str) -> Optional[dict]:            
      659 +    """Light status lookup for the poll endpoint: {status, ski
          +p_reason} or None."""                                         
      660 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      661 +        r = conn.execute("SELECT status, skip_reason FROM enti
          +ties WHERE entity_id=?",                                      
      662 +                         (entity_id,)).fetchone()             
      663 +        return dict(r) if r else None                         
      664 +                                                              
      665 +                                                              
      666 +def computing_count() -> int:                                 
      667 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      668 +        return conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM entities WHE
          +RE status='computing'").fetchone()[0]                         
      669 +                                                              
      670 +                                                              
      671 +def mark_computing(entity_id: str, entry_id: str, uniprot: Opt
          +ional[str], run_id: int) -> None:                             
      672 +    """Placeholder row so the poll can see 'computing' immedia
          +tely; never clobbers a real result."""                        
      673 +    with connect() as conn:                                   
      674 +        conn.execute(                                         
      675 +            "INSERT INTO entities (entity_id, entry_id, unipro
          +t, status, run_id, processed_at) "                            
      676 +            "VALUES (?,?,?,'computing',?,?) "                 
      677 +            "ON CONFLICT(entity_id) DO UPDATE SET status='comp
          +uting', run_id=excluded.run_id, "                             
      678 +            "processed_at=excluded.processed_at "             
      679 +            "WHERE entities.status NOT IN ('compared','screene
          +d','calculated')",                                            
      680 +            (entity_id, entry_id, uniprot, run_id, _now()))   
      681 +                                                              
      682 +                                                              
      683  def run_kind(label: str) -> Optional[str]:
      684      """'weekly' | 'backfill' | None — lets the release page ti
           tle itself 'Release month' vs
      685      'Release week' for a given label."""

      139 +def human_entities_of_entry(entry_id: str) -> list[str]:      
      140 +    """The human protein polymer_entity ids of one PDB entry (
          +any deposit date). Empty if the entry                         
      141 +    doesn't exist, isn't human, or has no protein entity — use
          +d by the Calculate tab to resolve a

          +s": {

      14 +def validate(raw: str) -> dict:                                
      15 +    """Resolve a user-entered PDB id / entity id to a computabl
         +e human entity, or explain why it                              
      16 +    can't be. Returns {'entity_id', 'in_index', 'post_cutoff', 
         +'others'} on success, else {'error'}.                          
      17 +    Does at most a couple of fast API calls (never the heavy co
         +mpute)."""                                                     
      18 +    raw = (raw or "").strip().upper()                          
      19 +    entry = raw.split("_")[0]                                  
      20 +    want = raw if "_" in raw else None                         
      21 +    if len(entry) != 4 or not entry.isalnum():                 
      22 +        return {"error": "Enter a 4-character PDB ID (optionall
         +y with an entity number, e.g. 7U14 or 7U14_1)."}               
      23 +                                                               
      24 +    idx = db.index_entities_for_entry(entry)          # index =
         + already-qualifying (human + AF model)                         
      25 +    if idx:                                                    
      26 +        row = next((r for r in idx if r["entity_id"] == want), 
         +None) or idx[0]                                                
      27 +        return {"entity_id": row["entity_id"], "in_index": True
         +, "post_cutoff": row["post_cutoff"],                           
      28 +                "others": [r["entity_id"] for r in idx]}       
      29 +                                                               
      30 +    ents = pdb.human_entities_of_entry(entry)         # not ind
         +exed -> live checks for a precise reason                       
      31 +    if not ents:                                               
      32 +        return {"error": f"{entry} isn't a human protein struct
         +ure in the PDB (no human protein "                             
      33 +                         "entity, or the entry doesn't exist)."
         +}                                                              
      34 +    if want and want not in ents:                              
      35 +        return {"error": f"{want} is not a human protein entity
         + of {entry}."}                                                 
      36 +    eid = want or ents[0]                                      
      37 +    meta = pdb.fetch_entity_metadata([eid]).get(eid)           
      38 +    if meta is None:                                           
      39 +        return {"error": f"Couldn't read metadata for {eid} fro
         +m the PDB."}                                                   
      40 +    if not meta.has_single_uniprot:                            
      41 +        return {"error": f"{eid} maps to no single UniProt (ant
         +ibody / fusion / chimera / synthetic); "                       
      42 +                         "AlphaFraud can't pick one AlphaFold m
         +odel to compare against."}                                     
      43 +    from . import calc_index                                   
      44 +    if not calc_index.af_has_model(meta.uniprot_accession):    
      45 +        return {"error": f"No AlphaFold model exists for UniPro
         +t {meta.uniprot_accession}, so there is "                      
      46 +                         "nothing to compare this structure aga
         +inst."}                                                        
      47 +    dep = (meta.deposit_date or "")[:10]                       
      48 +    return {"entity_id": eid, "in_index": False,               
      49 +            "post_cutoff": 1 if dep and dep >= config.AF_TRAINI
         +NG_CUTOFF.isoformat() else 0,                                  
      50 +            "others": ents}                                    
      51 +                                                               
      52 +                                                               
      53  def _store_annotation(entity: dict) -> None:
      54      """Fetch + store CATH / SCOP / citation for one entity (the
           /analysis 'enrich' loop, for a
      55      single 'calculated' row that the batch enricher — which onl
          y scans 'compared' — would skip)."""

          +.get("pdb") if request.is_json

          +ror"]})

      316 +        if st and st["status"] in ("compared", "screened", "ca
          +lculated"):                                                   
      317 +            return jsonify({"status": "ready", "entity_id": ei
          +d})

      322 +                            "reason": "The server is computing
          + other structures right now — please try again in a moment."})
      323 +        # Trigger the heavy compute in an isolated subprocess

          +culate_run_id())

      328 +        script = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "Alp
          +haFraud.py"                                                   
      329 +        subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(script), "calcul
          +ate", eid],                                                   
      330 +                         cwd=str(script.parent),              
      331 +                         stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=sub
          +process.DEVNULL,                                              
      332 +                         start_new_session=True)              
      333 +        return jsonify({"status": "computing", "entity_id": ei
          +d})

      560 +/* ---- Calculate tab: autocomplete + on-demand compute ---- *
          +/                                                             
      561 +(function () {                                                
      562 +  var input = document.getElementById("calcInput");           
      563 +  if (!input) return;                                         
      564 +  var go = document.getElementById("calcGo"),                 
      565 +      list = document.getElementById("calcSuggest"),          
      566 +      msg = document.getElementById("calcMsg"),               
      567 +      result = document.getElementById("calcResult"),         
      568 +      items = [], active = -1, timer = null, poll = null;     
      569 +                                                              
      570 +  function show(text, kind) {                                 
      571 +    msg.textContent = text;                                   
      572 +    msg.className = "calc-msg" + (kind ? " " + kind : "");    
      573 +    msg.hidden = !text;                                       
      574 +  }                                                           
      575 +  function hideList() { list.hidden = true; list.innerHTML = "
          +"; items = []; active = -1; }                                 
      576 +                                                              
      577 +  function render() {                                         
      578 +    list.innerHTML = "";                                      
      579 +    items.forEach(function (it, i) {                          
      580 +      var li = document.createElement("li");                  
      581 +      li.className = "calc-sug" + (i === active ? " on" : "");
      582 +      li.setAttribute("role", "option");                      
      583 +      li.innerHTML = '<b>' + it.entry_id + '</b> <span class="
          +calc-sug-t">' +                                               
      584 +        ((it.gene || "").replace("_HUMAN", "") + (it.title ? "
          + · " + it.title : "")) + '</span>' +                          
      585 +        (it.post_cutoff ? '' : ' <span class="calc-sug-pre">pr
          +e-cutoff</span>');                                            
      586 +      li.addEventListener("mousedown", function (e) { e.preven
          +tDefault(); choose(it); });                                   
      587 +      list.appendChild(li);                                   
      588 +    });                                                       
      589 +    list.hidden = items.length === 0;                         
      590 +  }                                                           
      591 +  function choose(it) { input.value = it.entry_id; hideList();
          + run(); }                                                     
      592 +                                                              
      593 +  function fetchSuggest() {                                   
      594 +    var q = input.value.trim();                               
      595 +    if (q.length < 1) { hideList(); return; }                 
      596 +    fetch("/api/calculate/suggest?q=" + encodeURIComponent(q))
      597 +      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })                
      598 +      .then(function (rows) { items = rows || []; active = ite
          +ms.length ? 0 : -1; render(); })                              
      599 +      .catch(function () { hideList(); });                    
      600 +  }                                                           
      601 +                                                              
      602 +  input.addEventListener("input", function () { clearTimeout(t
          +imer); timer = setTimeout(fetchSuggest, 140); });             
      603 +  input.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {            
      604 +    if (e.key === "ArrowDown" && items.length) { e.preventDefa
          +ult(); active = (active + 1) % items.length; render(); }      
      605 +    else if (e.key === "ArrowUp" && items.length) { e.preventD
          +efault(); active = (active - 1 + items.length) % items.length;
          + render(); }                                                  
      606 +    else if (e.key === "Tab" && items.length && active >= 0) {
          + e.preventDefault(); input.value = items[active].entry_id; hid
          +eList(); }                                                    
      607 +    else if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); if (!lis
          +t.hidden && active >= 0) choose(items[active]); else run(); } 
      608 +    else if (e.key === "Escape") { hideList(); }              
      609 +  });                                                         
      610 +  document.addEventListener("click", function (e) { if (!list.
          +contains(e.target) && e.target !== input) hideList(); });     
      611 +  go.addEventListener("click", run);                          
      612 +                                                              
      613 +  function run() {                                            
      614 +    var pdb = input.value.trim();                             
      615 +    if (!pdb) return;                                         
      616 +    hideList();                                               
      617 +    if (poll) { clearInterval(poll); poll = null; }           
      618 +    result.innerHTML = "";                                    
      619 +    show("Checking " + pdb.toUpperCase() + " …", "info");     
      620 +    fetch("/calculate/run", {                                 
      621 +      method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/
          +json" },                                                      
      622 +      body: JSON.stringify({ pdb: pdb })                      
      623 +    }).then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(handle)   
      624 +      .catch(function () { show("Something went wrong contacti
          +ng the server.", "err"); });                                  
      625 +  }                                                           
      626 +                                                              
      627 +  function handle(res) {                                      
      628 +    if (res.status === "error") { show(res.reason || "This ent
          +ry does not meet the criteria.", "err"); return; }            
      629 +    if (res.status === "busy") { show(res.reason, "warn"); ret
          +urn; }                                                        
      630 +    if (res.status === "ready") { loadPanel(res.entity_id); re
          +turn; }                                                       
      631 +    if (res.status === "computing") {                         
      632 +      show("Computing " + res.entity_id + " — fetching the Alp
          +haFold model, superposing and scoring. This can take up to a m
          +inute…", "info");                                             
      633 +      startPoll(res.entity_id);                               
      634 +    }                                                         
      635 +  }                                                           
      636 +  function startPoll(eid) {                                   
      637 +    var tries = 0;                                            
      638 +    poll = setInterval(function () {                          
      639 +      tries++;                                                
      640 +      fetch("/api/calculate/status/" + encodeURIComponent(eid)
          +)                                                             
      641 +        .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })              
      642 +        .then(function (s) {                                  
      643 +          if (s.status === "ready") { clearInterval(poll); pol
          +l = null; loadPanel(eid); }                                   
      644 +          else if (s.status === "error") { clearInterval(poll)
          +; poll = null; show(s.reason || "Could not process this struct
          +ure.", "err"); }                                              
      645 +          else if (tries > 60) { clearInterval(poll); poll = n
          +ull; show("Still working — this structure is taking unusually 
          +long. Try again shortly.", "warn"); }                         
      646 +        });                                                   
      647 +    }, 2000);                                                 
      648 +  }                                                           
      649 +  function loadPanel(eid) {                                   
      650 +    show("", null);                                           
      651 +    fetch("/calculate/panel/" + encodeURIComponent(eid))      
      652 +      .then(function (r) { return r.text(); })                
      653 +      .then(function (html) {                                 
      654 +        result.innerHTML = html;                              
      655 +        if (window.initExampleViewers) window.initExampleViewe
          +rs(result);                                                   
      656 +        result.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "st
          +art" });                                                      
      657 +      })                                                      
      658 +      .catch(function () { show("Computed, but the panel faile
          +d to load. Refresh and try again.", "err"); });               
      659 +  }                                                           
      660 +})();

  What's live

  A new "Calculate" nav tab — type any human PDB ID and get the same panel the
  auto examples produce, computed on demand with zero LLM usage.

  Tested end-to-end on the droplet:

  ┌───────────────────┬─────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────┐
  │       Input       │            Path             │        Result         │
  ├───────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │ 6TT5 (Artemis, in │ reuse                       │ instant ready         │
  │  corpus)          │                             │                       │
  ├───────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │ 1UBQ (ubiquitin,  │                             │ TM 0.96 → green       │
  │ pre-cutoff, new)  │ full compute                │ "match" panel +       │
  │                   │                             │ pre-cutoff note       │
  ├───────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │                   │ resolves to the protein     │ TM 0.98, panel HTTP   │
  │ 1TUP (p53)        │ entity 1TUP_3 (not the DNA  │ 200                   │
  │                   │ chains), computes           │                       │
  ├───────────────────┼─────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────┤
  │ xx                │ validation                  │ correct reason        │
  │                   │                             │ returned              │
  └───────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────┘

  How it works (all reusing existing pieces):
  - Strict autocomplete index (calculate_index) — seeded instantly from the
  82,796 corpus entities that already have a confirmed AlphaFold model; the
  pre-cutoff human sweep is running now to complete it; and the weekly run() now
  refreshes it automatically (the feasibility condition you set — confirmed
  working, it's hooked beside the weekly-examples rebuild).
  - Validation with specific reasons — not found / not human / antibody-fusion
  (no single UniProt) / no AlphaFold model, each surfaced to the user.
  - On-demand compute — pipeline.process_entity in an isolated subprocess
  (avoids the biotite/tmtools thread-safety issue), stored as
  status='calculated' so it's excluded from every aggregate
  (home/leaderboard/analysis/archive/examples unchanged — verified), then the
  frontend polls and injects the panel.
  - examples_auto.build now branches: good predictions (TM ≥ 0.7) render the
  green "why AlphaFold gets it right" panel like the Artemis control; the rest
  keep the failure framing. Pre-cutoff entries get a note that AlphaFold may
  have trained on them.

  New: calc_index.py, calculate.py, calculate.html; modified: db.py, pdb.py,
  pipeline.py, webapp.py (5 routes), app.js (autocomplete + poll + inject),
  examples_auto.py, base.html, _expanel.html, AlphaFraud.py (calc-index,
  calculate subcommands).

  Note on the background sweep: it enumerates every pre-cutoff human protein
  entity (a large one-off, ~tens of thousands), so it'll run for a while; the
  autocomplete already works for the whole post-cutoff set and grows to cover
  pre-cutoff entries as it finishes. It's idempotent, so if it dies it can be
  re-run with AlphaFraud.py calc-index. Once it's done I'd suggest a quick visit
  to alphafraud.mdeller.com/calculate to try the live typeahead in a browser
  (the one thing I can't exercise from here).

⏺ Now the Architecture & workflow section (with pseudocode), placed after
  Features:

⏺ Update(README.md)
  ⎿  Added 58 lines
       37  | Branded, mobile-responsive report | Flask app with the signa
           ture scatter, heatmaps, per-domain tables and an all-time lead
           erboard; every plot has a CSV export and a plain-language expl
           anatory panel that defines each metric twice over (a lay sente
           nce and the underlying equation), and every table exports to C
           SV |
       38  | One-command deploy | Provisioning, deploy and one-shot relea
           se scripts for a DigitalOcean droplet (gunicorn, nginx, certbo
           t TLS) |
       39  
       40 +## 🏗️ Architecture and workflow                               
       41 +                                                              
       42 +One controller (`AlphaFraud.py`, subcommands) drives a small P
          +ython package; a Flask app serves the results. Two runtimes sh
          +are one SQLite file: a **systemd timer** runs the weekly pipel
          +ine (writes the database and the ribbon/coords assets), and an
          + always-on **gunicorn** process serves the site by reading tha
          +t same database live.                                         
       43 +                                                              
       44 +| Module | Responsibility |                                   
       45 +|---|---|                                                     
       46 +| `pdb.py` | RCSB Search (new human protein entities) + Data G
          +raphQL (UniProt mapping, dates, method) |                     
       47 +| `afdb.py` | AlphaFold DB fetch: the blind model + PAE, choos
          +ing the fragment covering the entity's span |                 
       48 +| `novelty.py` | max % identity to any pre-cutoff chain (so ge
          +nuinely unseen sequences are flagged) |                       
       49 +| `compare.py` | the full metric suite on the aligned span (TM
          +, lDDT, GDT, RMSD, per-residue deviation, PAE honesty) |      
       50 +| `ribbon.py` | Cα-deviation SVG + 3D-viewer coords + the Alph
          +aFold "ghost" (vendored, offline) |                           
       51 +| `annotate.py` / `analysis.py` | CATH / SCOP / citation enric
          +hment and the `/analysis` snapshot (enrichment, clustering, PC
          +A) |                                                          
       52 +| `examples_auto.py` | the deterministic, no-LLM panel builder
          + (match **or** failure framing) |                             
       53 +| `calc_index.py` / `calculate.py` | the Calculate tab's stric
          +t autocomplete index + on-demand single-entity compute |      
       54 +| `db.py` / `report.py` / `webapp.py` | SQLite persistence, Pl
          +otly figure JSON, and the Flask routes |                      
       55 +                                                              
       56 +**The weekly pipeline** (`AlphaFraud.py run`, one entity at a 
          +time):                                                        
       57 +                                                              
       58 +```text                                                       
       59 +for entity in RCSB.search(human, protein, deposited_after = 20
          +18-04-30):                                                    
       60 +    if entity in db: continue                                 
       61 +    meta = RCSB.graphql(entity)                       # UniPro
          +t, dates, method, chains                                      
       62 +    if not meta.single_uniprot: skip("antibody / fusion / chim
          +era"); continue                                               
       63 +    af = AlphaFoldDB.model(meta.uniprot)              # the bl
          +ind prediction (fragment covering the span)                   
       64 +    if af is None:               skip("no AlphaFold model"); c
          +ontinue                                                       
       65 +    exp, model = download(entity), download(af)               
       66 +    m        = compare(exp, model)                    # TM, lD
          +DT, GDT, RMSD, per-residue dev, PAE honesty                   
       67 +    novelty  = max_identity_to_pre_cutoff(meta.sequence)      
       68 +    fraud    = mean_i( pLDDT_i/100 * clamp(dev_i, 0, 15Å) / 15
          + )   # confidence-weighted error                              
       69 +    confidently_wrong = (mean_pLDDT > 70) and (TM < 0.5)      
          +    # sure, and still wrong                                   
       70 +    ribbon.render(exp, model)                         # deviat
          +ion SVG + coords + AF ghost                                   
       71 +    db.store(entity, m, novelty, fraud, confidently_wrong)    
       72 +                                                              
       73 +analysis.rebuild()            # /analysis enrichment snapshot 
       74 +rebuild_weekly_examples()     # auto 'example of the week' + w
          +eekly archive                                                 
       75 +calc_index.refresh(metas)     # keep the Calculate autocomplet
          +e index current                                               
       76 +```                                                           
       77 +                                                              
       78 +**The always-on web app** (gunicorn) reads the same SQLite and
          + renders cached Plotly JSON:                                  
       79 +                                                              
       80 +```text                                                       
       81 +GET /            -> cumulative "fraud quadrant" dashboard (fig
          +ures cached, short TTL)                                       
       82 +GET /entry/<id>  -> per-structure heatmaps, calibration, per-d
          +omain metrics                                                 
       83 +GET /examples    -> positive control + auto example-of-the-wee
          +k + curated deep-dives                                        
       84 +GET /calculate   -> type a human PDB id  ->  (reuse if known |
          + compute on demand)  ->  panel                                
       85 +```                                                           
       86 +                                                              
       87 +**The Calculate tab** reuses the very same `process_entity()` 
          +as the pipeline, run in an isolated subprocess so a heavy comp
          +utation never blocks a web worker, and stored apart so it neve
          +r pollutes the science:                                       
       88 +                                                              
       89 +```text                                                       
       90 +POST /calculate/run { pdb }:                                  
       91 +    entity = resolve_human_entity(pdb)                # strict
          + index, else a live RCSB check                                
       92 +    if not qualifies:  return reason                 # not hum
          +an / no single UniProt / no AF model                          
       93 +    if entity already computed:  return "ready"      # cached 
          +-> instant                                                    
       94 +    mark(entity, "computing"); spawn AlphaFraud.py calculate <
          +entity>   # same pipeline, subprocess                         
       95 +    return "computing"                               # the bro
          +wser polls, then injects the panel

      322  | **Biology & disease** | Central coagulation zymogen; activat

      325 +## 🧪 Calculate                                               
      326 +                                                              
      327 +The **[Calculate](https://alphafraud.mdeller.com/calculate)** 
          +tab runs the whole pipeline on demand for any human PDB ID you
          + type and returns the same panel format as the auto examples —
          + KPIs, the Cα-deviation ribbon, the interactive 3D viewer and 
          +a deterministic write-up — with **no AI-generated text**.     
      328 +                                                              
      329 +- **Strict autocomplete.** The input only completes to entries
          + that (1) exist, (2) are human, and (3) have an AlphaFold mode
          +l. That comes from a precomputed index — seeded for free from 
          +the corpus (the ~83k entities already carrying a confirmed Alp
          +haFold model), back-filled with a one-off pre-cutoff human swe
          +ep, and refreshed automatically by every weekly run. Press Tab
          + to complete.                                                 
      330 +- **Any human structure, blind or not.** Post-cutoff entries a
          +re genuine blind tests; pre-cutoff entries compute too, with a
          + note that AlphaFold may have seen them in training (so it is 
          +not a blind prediction).                                      
      331 +- **Clear reasons on rejection.** If an entry doesn't qualify 
          +you are told exactly why: not a human protein structure, no si
          +ngle UniProt mapping (antibody / fusion / chimera / synthetic)
          +, or no AlphaFold model for the sequence.                     
      332 +- **Right or wrong, honestly.** A good prediction renders the 
          +green "AlphaFold gets it right" panel (like the positive contr
          +ol); a confidently-wrong one renders the failure panel — the s
          +ame deterministic builder (`examples_auto.build`) decides whic
          +h from the metrics.                                           
      333 +- **Isolated and cached.** On-demand results are stored under 
          +a separate run and status, so they never enter the leaderboard
          +, stats, analysis or archive; the heavy compute runs in an iso
          +lated subprocess; and a repeat request for the same structure 
          +is instant.                                                   
      334 +                                                              
      335  ## 🧮 Coverage and skipped entities
      336  
      337  Not every deposited entity can be compared. AlphaFold DB holds
            a single model per human UniProt sequence (monomers only), so
            anything that does not map cleanly to one accession, has no m
           odel covering its resolved range, or is too small or too large
            to compare is recorded as *skipped* rather than forced throug
           h.

      568          return False
      569  
      570  
      571 +# ------------------------------------------------------------
          +--------------------------                                    
      572 +# Statistics page helpers                                     
      573 +# ------------------------------------------------------------
          +--------------------------                                    
      574 +def _fmt_int(v) -> str:                                       
      575 +    return "—" if v is None else (f"{v:,}" if isinstance(v, (i
          +nt, float)) else str(v))                                      
      576 +                                                              
      577 +                                                              
      578 +def _fmt_bytes(v) -> str:                                     
      579 +    if not v:                                                 
      580 +        return "—"                                            
      581 +    v = float(v)                                              
      582 +    for u in ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB"):                   
      583 +        if v < 1024:                                          
      584 +            return f"{int(v)} {u}" if u == "B" else f"{v:.1f} 
          +{u}"                                                          
      585 +        v /= 1024                                             
      586 +    return f"{v:.1f} PB"                                      
      587 +                                                              
      588 +                                                              
      589 +def _fmt_dur(secs) -> str:                                    
      590 +    if not secs:                                              
      591 +        return "—"                                            
      592 +    s = int(secs)                                             
      593 +    d, s = divmod(s, 86400)                                   
      594 +    h, s = divmod(s, 3600)                                    
      595 +    m, _ = divmod(s, 60)                                      
      596 +    return f"{d}d {h}h" if d else (f"{h}h {m}m" if h else f"{m
          +}m")                                                          
      597 +                                                              
      598 +                                                              
      599 +def _fmt_ts(ts) -> str:                                       
      600 +    return ts.replace("T", " ")[:16] if ts else "—"           
      601 +                                                              
      602 +                                                              
      603 +def _system_metrics() -> dict:                                
      604 +    """Live host/process metrics (Linux droplet); each probe i
          +s guarded so it degrades gracefully                           
      605 +    off-server (returns what it can)."""                      
      606 +    import platform                                           
      607 +    import shutil                                             
      608 +    import subprocess                                         
      609 +    m = {"python": platform.python_version(), "app": __version
          +__}                                                           
      610 +    try:                                                      
      611 +        m["cpu"] = os.cpu_count()                             
      612 +    except Exception:                                         
      613 +        pass                                                  
      614 +    try:                                                      
      615 +        m["load"] = os.getloadavg()                           
      616 +    except Exception:                                         
      617 +        pass                                                  
      618 +    try:                                                      
      619 +        mem = {}                                              
      620 +        with open("/proc/meminfo") as fh:                     
      621 +            for line in fh:                                   
      622 +                parts = line.split(":")                       
      623 +                if len(parts) == 2:                           
      624 +                    mem[parts[0]] = int(parts[1].split()[0]) *
          + 1024                                                         
      625 +        m["mem_total"], m["mem_avail"] = mem.get("MemTotal"), 
          +mem.get("MemAvailable")                                       
      626 +        if m["mem_total"] and m["mem_avail"]:                 
      627 +            m["mem_used"] = m["mem_total"] - m["mem_avail"]   
      628 +    except Exception:                                         
      629 +        pass                                                  
      630 +    try:                                                      
      631 +        with open("/proc/self/status") as fh:                 
      632 +            for line in fh:                                   
      633 +                if line.startswith("VmRSS:"):                 
      634 +                    m["rss"] = int(line.split()[1]) * 1024    
      635 +                    break                                     
      636 +    except Exception:                                         
      637 +        pass                                                  
      638 +    try:                                                      
      639 +        with open("/proc/uptime") as fh:                      
      640 +            m["uptime"] = float(fh.read().split()[0])         
      641 +    except Exception:                                         
      642 +        pass                                                  
      643 +    try:                                                      
      644 +        du = shutil.disk_usage(str(config.DATA_DIR))          
      645 +        m["disk_total"], m["disk_used"], m["disk_free"] = du.t
          +otal, du.used, du.free                                        
      646 +    except Exception:                                         
      647 +        pass                                                  
      648 +                                                              
      649 +    def _du(path):                                            
      650 +        try:                                                  
      651 +            r = subprocess.run(["du", "-sb", str(path)], captu
          +re_output=True, text=True, timeout=25)                        
      652 +            return int(r.stdout.split()[0]) if r.returncode ==
          + 0 else None                                                  
      653 +        except Exception:                                     
      654 +            return None                                       
      655 +    m["ribbon_bytes"] = _du(config.RIBBON_DIR)                
      656 +    m["struct_bytes"] = _du(config.STRUCT_CACHE)              
      657 +    m["data_bytes"] = _du(config.DATA_DIR)                    
      658 +    try:                                                      
      659 +        m["ribbon_count"] = sum(1 for _ in config.RIBBON_DIR.g
          +lob("*.svg"))                                                 
      660 +    except Exception:                                         
      661 +        pass                                                  
      662 +    return m                                                  
      663 +                                                              
      664 +                                                              
      665  def _example_highlights():
      666      """Overlay points for the 9 curated deep-dive examples on 
           the home scatter: the accurate
      667      control (green) + the eight failures (red), each linking t
           o its Examples-tab panel."""

  load:

  screened (67,314), confidently-wrong + rate, novel + rate, novel+CW + rate,
  distinct proteins, mean TM / lDDT / pLDDT, skipped, errors

  backfill months (117), weekly runs, AlphaFold cutoff, last update, next PDB
  release

  weekly run, ribbons rendered (15,451), on-demand calculations, currently
  computing
  - 🖥️ Server — app/Python version, CPU cores, load average (1/5/15), system
  memory used/total, this worker's RSS, uptime
  - 💾 Storage & database — DB size (6.1 GB), ribbons (15,451 files · 4.1 GB),
  structure cache (9.7 GB), data dir total, disk used/free/total, and per-table
  row counts
  - 🔌 Data sources & APIs — RCSB Search / Data GraphQL / files / AlphaFold DB
  (all linked), the Calculate index size, AlphaFold-availability checks cached,
  source-code link
  - 👥 Traffic & coverage — unique visitors, page views, visitors today, total
  processed, archive coverage

  It's wired into the nav (with its own teal accent, matching the tab-colour
  scheme), and the small header stats panel now has a "Full statistics →" link
  down to it. Everything the header panel shows is included and greatly
  expanded.

  (now 134,107 qualifying human entries autocompletable), and the server metrics
  confirm the box is comfortable (770 MB of 3.8 GB used, load ~0.2).

  fast now, but if the page ever feels sluggish under traffic I can cache those
  filesystem figures with a short TTL. Want me to add this to the README + To Do
  (and commit) like the other tabs, or leave the docs until you've had a look?
